(E.6)

\displaystyle+2\left[\left(p_{31}^{\nu_{1}}p_{21}^{2}-p_{21}^{\nu_{1}}p_{31}^{2}-4m^{2}p_{32}^{\nu_{1}}\right)J_{3}+p_{21}^{\nu_{1}}J_{2}\left(p_{21}\right)-p_{32}^{\nu_{1}}J_{2}\left(p_{32}\right)\right]

\displaystyle-\left(T^{APS}\right)^{\nu_{1}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\left[P_{31}^{\nu_{2}}\Delta_{3\nu_{2}}^{\nu_{1}}+(p_{21}^{\nu_{1}}-p_{32}^{\nu_{1}})I_{\log}\right]-4\left(p_{21}\cdot p_{32}\right)J_{3}^{\nu_{1}}

(E.7)

\displaystyle+2\left[\left(p_{31}^{\nu_{1}}p_{21}^{2}-p_{21}^{\nu_{1}}p_{31}^{2}+4m^{2}p_{21}^{\nu_{1}}\right)J_{3}+p_{21}^{\nu_{1}}J_{2}\left(p_{21}\right)-p_{32}^{\nu_{1}}J_{2}\left(p_{32}\right)\right]

\displaystyle-\left(T^{VSS}\right)^{\nu_{1}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\left[P_{31}^{\nu_{2}}\Delta_{3\nu_{2}}^{\nu_{1}}+(p_{21}^{\nu_{1}}-p_{32}^{\nu_{1}})I_{\log}\right]-4\left(p_{21}\cdot p_{32}+4m^{2}\right)J_{3}^{\nu_{1}}

(E.8)

\displaystyle+2\left[\left(p_{31}^{\nu_{1}}p_{21}^{2}-p_{21}^{\nu_{1}}p_{31}^{2}-4m^{2}p_{31}^{\nu_{1}}\right)J_{3}+p_{21}^{\nu_{1}}J_{2}\left(p_{21}\right)-p_{32}^{\nu_{1}}J_{2}\left(p_{32}\right)\right]

Second version:

\displaystyle\left(t^{SAP}\right)^{\nu_{1}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left[K_{1}^{\nu_{1}}S_{23}+K_{2}^{\nu_{1}}\left(S_{13}+2m^{2}\right)-K_{3}^{\nu_{1}}\left(S_{12}+2m^{2}\right)\right]\frac{1}{D_{123}}

(E.9)

\displaystyle\left(t^{PVP}\right)^{\nu_{1}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left[-K_{1}^{\nu_{1}}S_{23}-K_{2}^{\nu_{1}}S_{13}+K_{3}^{\nu_{1}}S_{12}\right]\frac{1}{D_{123}}

(E.10)

\displaystyle\left(t^{PAS}\right)^{\nu_{1}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\left[K_{1}^{\nu_{1}}\left(S_{23}+2m^{2}\right)+K_{2}^{\nu_{1}}S_{13}-K_{3}^{\nu_{1}}\left(S_{12}+2m^{2}\right)\right]\frac{1}{D_{123}}

(E.11)

\displaystyle\left(t^{SVS}\right)^{\nu_{1}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left[K_{1}^{\nu_{1}}\left(S_{23}+2m^{2}\right)+K_{2}^{\nu_{1}}\left(S_{13}+2m^{2}\right)-K_{3}^{\nu_{1}}S_{12}\right]\frac{1}{D_{123}}

(E.12)

\displaystyle-\left(T^{SAP}\right)^{\nu_{1}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\left[P_{21}^{\nu_{2}}\Delta_{3\nu_{2}}^{\nu_{1}}+\left(p_{32}^{\nu_{1}}+p_{31}^{\nu_{1}}\right)I_{\log}\right]+4\left(p_{32}\cdot p_{31}\right)J_{3}^{\nu_{1}}

(E.13)

\displaystyle+2\left[\left(p_{21}^{\nu_{1}}p_{31}^{2}-p_{31}^{\nu_{1}}p_{21}^{2}+4m^{2}p_{32}^{\nu_{1}}\right)J_{3}+p_{32}^{\nu_{1}}J_{2}\left(p_{32}\right)+p_{31}^{\nu_{1}}J_{2}\left(p_{31}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\left(T^{PVP}\right)^{\nu_{1}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\left[P_{21}^{\nu_{2}}\Delta_{3\nu_{2}}^{\nu_{1}}+\left(p_{32}^{\nu_{1}}+p_{31}^{\nu_{1}}\right)I_{\log}\right]+4\left(p_{32}\cdot p_{31}\right)J_{3}^{\nu_{1}}

(E.14)

\displaystyle+2\left[\left(p_{21}^{\nu_{1}}p_{31}^{2}-p_{31}^{\nu_{1}}p_{21}^{2}\right)J_{3}+p_{32}^{\nu_{1}}J_{2}\left(p_{32}\right)+p_{31}^{\nu_{1}}J_{2}\left(p_{31}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\left(T^{PAS}\right)^{\nu_{1}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\left[P_{21}^{\nu_{2}}\Delta_{3\nu_{2}}^{\nu_{1}}+\left(p_{32}^{\nu_{1}}+p_{31}^{\nu_{1}}\right)I_{\log}\right]+4\left(p_{32}\cdot p_{31}\right)J_{3}^{\nu_{1}}

(E.15)

\displaystyle+2\left[\left(p_{21}^{\nu_{1}}p_{31}^{2}-p_{31}^{\nu_{1}}p_{21}^{2}+4m^{2}p_{31}^{\nu_{1}}\right)J_{3}+p_{32}^{\nu_{1}}J_{2}\left(p_{32}\right)+p_{31}^{\nu_{1}}J_{2}\left(p_{31}\right)\right]

\displaystyle-\left(T^{SVS}\right)^{\nu_{1}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\left[P_{21}^{\nu_{2}}\Delta_{3\nu_{2}}^{\nu_{1}}+\left(p_{32}^{\nu_{1}}+p_{31}^{\nu_{1}}\right)I_{\log}\right]+4\left(p_{32}\cdot p_{31}-4m^{2}\right)J_{3}^{\nu_{1}}

(E.16)

\displaystyle+2\left[\left(p_{21}^{\nu_{1}}p_{31}^{2}-p_{31}^{\nu_{1}}p_{21}^{2}-4m^{2}p_{21}^{\nu_{1}}\right)J_{3}+p_{32}^{\nu_{1}}J_{2}\left(p_{32}\right)+p_{31}^{\nu_{1}}J_{2}\left(p_{31}\right)\right]

Third version:

\displaystyle\left(t^{SPA}\right)^{\nu_{1}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left[K_{1}^{\nu_{1}}\left(S_{23}+2m^{2}\right)-K_{2}^{\nu_{1}}\left(S_{13}+2m^{2}\right)-K_{3}^{\nu_{1}}S_{12}\right]\frac{1}{D_{123}}

(E.17)

\displaystyle\left(t^{PSA}\right)^{\nu_{1}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left[-K_{1}^{\nu_{1}}\left(S_{23}+2m^{2}\right)+K_{2}^{\nu_{1}}S_{13}+K_{3}^{\nu_{1}}\left(S_{12}+2m^{2}\right)\right]\frac{1}{D_{123}}

(E.18)

\displaystyle\left(t^{PPV}\right)^{\nu_{1}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\left[-K_{1}^{\nu_{1}}S_{23}+K_{2}^{\nu_{1}}S_{13}+K_{3}^{\nu_{1}}S_{12}\right]\frac{1}{D_{123}}

(E.19)

\displaystyle\left(t^{SSV}\right)^{\nu_{1}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left[-K_{1}^{\nu_{1}}S_{23}+K_{2}^{\nu_{1}}\left(S_{13}+2m^{2}\right)+K_{3}^{\nu_{1}}\left(S_{12}+2m^{2}\right)\right]\frac{1}{D_{123}}

(E.20)

\displaystyle\left(T^{SPA}\right)^{\nu_{1}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\left[P_{32}^{\nu_{2}}\Delta_{3\nu_{2}}^{\nu_{1}}-\left(p_{21}^{\nu_{1}}+p_{31}^{\nu_{1}}\right)I_{\log}\right]+4\left(p_{21}\cdot p_{31}\right)J_{3}^{\nu_{1}}

(E.21)

\displaystyle+2\left[\left(p_{31}^{\nu_{1}}p_{21}^{2}+p_{21}^{\nu_{1}}p_{31}^{2}-4m^{2}p_{21}^{\nu_{1}}\right)J_{3}-p_{21}^{\nu_{1}}J_{2}\left(p_{21}\right)-p_{31}^{\nu_{1}}J_{2}\left(p_{31}\right)\right]

\displaystyle-\left(T^{PSA}\right)^{\nu_{1}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\left[P_{32}^{\nu_{2}}\Delta_{3\nu_{2}}^{\nu_{1}}-\left(p_{21}^{\nu_{1}}+p_{31}^{\nu_{1}}\right)I_{\log}\right]+4\left(p_{21}\cdot p_{31}\right)J_{3}^{\nu_{1}}

(E.22)

\displaystyle+2\left[\left(p_{31}^{\nu_{1}}p_{21}^{2}+p_{21}^{\nu_{1}}p_{31}^{2}-4m^{2}p_{31}^{\nu_{1}}\right)J_{3}-p_{21}^{\nu_{1}}J_{2}\left(p_{21}\right)-p_{31}^{\nu_{1}}J_{2}\left(p_{31}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\left(T^{PPV}\right)^{\nu_{1}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\left[P_{32}^{\nu_{2}}\Delta_{3\nu_{2}}^{\nu_{1}}-\left(p_{21}^{\nu_{1}}+p_{31}^{\nu_{1}}\right)I_{\log}\right]+4\left(p_{21}\cdot p_{31}\right)J_{3}^{\nu_{1}}

(E.23)

\displaystyle+2\left[\left(p_{31}^{\nu_{1}}p_{21}^{2}+p_{21}^{\nu_{1}}p_{31}^{2}\right)J_{3}-p_{21}^{\nu_{1}}J_{2}\left(p_{21}\right)-p_{31}^{\nu_{1}}J_{2}\left(p_{31}\right)\right]

\displaystyle-\left(T^{SSV}\right)^{\nu_{1}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\left[P_{32}^{\nu_{2}}\Delta_{3\nu_{2}}^{\nu_{1}}-\left(p_{21}^{\nu_{1}}+p_{31}^{\nu_{1}}\right)I_{\log}\right]+4\left(p_{21}\cdot p_{31}-4m^{2}\right)J_{3}^{\nu_{1}}

(E.24)

\displaystyle+2\left[\left(p_{31}^{\nu_{1}}p_{21}^{2}+p_{21}^{\nu_{1}}p_{31}^{2}-4m^{2}\left(p_{21}^{\nu_{1}}+p_{31}^{\nu_{1}}\right)\right)J_{3}-p_{21}^{\nu_{1}}J_{2}\left(p_{21}\right)-p_{31}^{\nu_{1}}J_{2}\left(p_{31}\right)\right]

Appendix F
Surface Terms

The surface terms used in this work appear in a totally symmetrical way in
the indices, for the first time treated from the point of view of the IReg
strategy. The meaning of the notation used is

g_{(\mu_{12}}g_{\mu_{34})}=g_{\mu_{12}}g_{\mu_{34}}+g_{\mu_{13}}g_{\mu_{24}}+g_{\mu_{14}}g_{\mu_{23}}.

(F.1)

For instance, in the case of permutations involving six indices as the
product of the metrics by the logarithmically divergent object
\Delta_{2\mu\nu},
we have forty-five terms given by,

\displaystyle g_{(\mu_{12}}g_{\mu_{34}}

(F.2)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Delta_{2\mu_{12}}g_{(\mu_{34}}g_{\mu_{56})}+\Delta_{2\mu_{13}}g_{(\mu_{24}}g_{\mu_{56})}+\Delta_{2\mu_{14}}g_{(\mu_{23}}g_{\mu_{56})}+\Delta_{2\mu_{15}}g_{(\mu_{23}}g_{\mu_{46})}+\Delta_{2\mu_{16}}g_{(\mu_{23}}g_{\mu_{45})}

\displaystyle+\Delta_{2\mu_{23}}g_{(\mu_{14}}g_{\mu_{56})}+\Delta_{2\mu_{24}}g_{(\mu_{13}}g_{\mu_{56})}+\Delta_{2\mu_{25}}g_{(\mu_{13}}g_{\mu_{46})}+\Delta_{2\mu_{26}}g_{(\mu_{13}}g_{\mu_{45})}

\displaystyle+\Delta_{2\mu_{34}}g_{(\mu_{12}}g_{\mu_{56})}+\Delta_{2\mu_{35}}g_{(\mu_{12}}g_{\mu_{46})}+\Delta_{2\mu_{36}}g_{(\mu_{12}}g_{\mu_{45})}

\displaystyle+\Delta_{2\mu_{45}}g_{(\mu_{12}}g_{\mu_{36})}+\Delta_{2\mu_{46}}g_{(\mu_{12}}g_{\mu_{35})}

\displaystyle+\Delta_{2\mu_{56}}g_{(\mu_{12}}g_{\mu_{34})}.

This can be written succinctly as

g_{(\mu_{12}}g_{\mu_{34}}\Delta_{2\mu_{56})}=\sum_{i_{2}>i_{1}=1}^{5}\Delta_{2i_{1}i_{2}}g_{(i_{3}i_{4}}g_{i_{5}i_{6})}\text{ with }i_{n}\not=i_{m}

(F.3)

where
i_{n}
denotes
\mu_{i_{n}}
. For the box terms we may also write

g_{(\mu_{1}\mu_{2}}\square_{3\mu_{3456})}=\sum_{i_{2}>i_{1}=1}^{5}g_{\mu_{i_{1}}\mu_{i_{2}}}\square_{3\mu_{i_{3}}\mu_{i_{4}}\mu_{i_{5}}\mu_{i_{6}}}.

(F.4)

F.1
Uniqueness Factor: Combination of the violating terms

As we saw, surface terms violate several symmetry relations. However, if the
relations are satisfied, relations between surface terms emerge for their
traces and the finite part. Through the strategy (
3
), we saw that
all the divergent objects were organized into standardized objects as to
their tensor degree and power counting. We have

\displaystyle\Xi_{\nu_{23}}^{\left(a\right)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left[2\square_{3\rho\nu_{23}}^{\rho}-2\Delta_{2\nu_{23}}-g_{\nu_{23}}\Delta_{2\rho}^{\rho}\right]

(F.5)

\displaystyle\Xi_{\alpha_{12}\nu_{23}}^{\left(b\right)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left[3\Sigma_{4\rho\alpha_{12}\nu_{23}}^{\rho}-8\square_{3\alpha_{12}\nu_{23}}-g_{\alpha_{12}\nu_{23}}\Delta_{2\rho}^{\rho}\right]

(F.6)

\displaystyle\Xi_{\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}}^{\mathrm{quad}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left[W_{2\rho\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}}^{\rho}-2\Delta_{1\alpha_{12}}+2g_{\alpha_{12}}I_{\text{{quad}}}-2m^{2}\left(\Delta_{2\alpha_{12}}+g_{\alpha_{12}}I_{\log}\right)\right].

(F.7)

In this way, this organization allows us to write the
U
-factor as

\displaystyle U_{\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{3}\theta_{\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}}\left(2\Delta_{2\rho}^{\rho}+i/\pi\right)+\frac{1}{9}\left(3P^{\nu_{2}}P^{\nu_{3}}+q^{\nu_{23}}\right)\Xi_{\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}\nu_{23}}^{b}

(F.8)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{18}\left(3P^{\nu_{2}}P^{\nu_{3}}+q^{\nu_{23}}\right)g_{(\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}}\Xi_{\nu_{2}\nu_{3})}^{a}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\left(P^{2}+q^{2}\right)\Xi_{\alpha_{12}}^{a}-P^{\nu_{1}}P_{(\alpha_{2}}\Xi_{\alpha_{1})\nu_{1}}^{a}+4\Xi^{\mathrm{quad}}

The uniqueness factor that arises in the basic permutations

U_{\alpha_{2}\nu_{1}}=\left(4\Upsilon_{\nu_{1}\alpha_{2}}+2q_{\nu_{1}}\Upsilon_{\alpha_{2}}+2q_{\alpha_{2}}\Upsilon_{\nu_{1}}+q_{\alpha_{2}}q_{\nu_{1}}\Upsilon\right),

(F.9)

its explicit expression reads

\displaystyle U_{\alpha_{2}\nu_{1}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{3}\theta_{\nu_{1}\alpha_{2}}\left(2\Delta_{2\rho}^{\rho}+i/\pi\right)

(F.10)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{9}\left(3P^{\nu_{2}}P^{\nu_{3}}+q^{\nu_{23}}\right)\left[3\Sigma_{4\rho\nu_{1}\alpha_{2}\nu_{23}}^{\rho}-8\square_{3\nu_{1}\alpha_{2}\nu_{23}}-g_{\nu_{1}\alpha_{2}\nu_{23}}\Delta_{2\rho}^{\rho}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{18}\left(3P^{\nu_{2}}P^{\nu_{3}}+q^{\nu_{23}}\right)\left[g_{(\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}}\left(2\square_{3\rho\nu_{23})}^{\rho}-2\Delta_{2\nu_{23})}-g_{\nu_{23})}\Delta_{2\rho}^{\rho}\right)\right]

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\left(P^{2}+q^{2}\right)\left[2\left(\square_{3\rho\nu_{1}\alpha_{2}}^{\rho}-\Delta_{2\nu_{1}\alpha_{2}}\right)-g_{\nu_{1}\alpha_{2}}\Delta_{2\rho}^{\rho}\right]

\displaystyle-P_{\alpha_{2}}P^{\nu_{2}}\left[2\left(\square_{3\rho\nu_{1}\nu_{2}}^{\rho}-\Delta_{2\nu_{1}\nu_{2}}\right)-g_{\nu_{1}\nu_{2}}\Delta_{2\rho}^{\rho}\right]

\displaystyle-P_{\nu_{1}}P^{\nu_{2}}\left[2\left(\square_{3\rho\alpha_{2}\nu_{2}}^{\rho}-\Delta_{2\alpha_{2}\nu_{2}}\right)-g_{\alpha_{2}\nu_{2}}\Delta_{2\rho}^{\rho}\right]

\displaystyle+4\left[W_{2\rho\nu_{1}\alpha_{2}}^{\rho}-2\Delta_{1\nu_{1}\alpha_{2}}+2g_{\nu_{1}\alpha_{2}}I_{\text{{quad}}}-2m^{2}\left(\Delta_{2\nu_{1}\alpha_{2}}+g_{\nu_{1}\alpha_{2}}I_{\log}\right)\right].

In the massless limit and independent of unique or vanishing surface terms

U_{\alpha_{2}\nu_{1}}=-\frac{1}{3}\theta_{\nu_{1}\alpha_{2}}\left(2\Delta_{2\rho}^{\rho}+\frac{i}{\pi}\right)=-\frac{1}{3}\theta_{\nu_{1}\alpha_{2}}\Upsilon

(F.11)

\displaystyle U_{\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{3}\theta_{\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}}\Upsilon+\frac{1}{9}\left(3P^{\nu_{12}}+q^{\nu_{12}}\right)\Xi_{\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}\nu_{12}}^{\left(b\right)}-P^{\nu_{1}}P_{(\alpha_{2}}\Xi_{\alpha_{1})\nu_{1}}^{\left(a\right)}

(F.12)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{18}\left(3P^{\nu_{12}}+q^{\nu_{12}}\right)g_{(\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}}\Xi_{1\nu_{12})}^{\left(a\right)}-\frac{1}{2}\left(P^{2}+q^{2}\right)\Xi_{\alpha_{12}}^{\left(a\right)}+4\Xi^{\mathrm{quad}},

where the definitions

\displaystyle\Xi_{\alpha_{12}}^{\mathrm{quad}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\square_{2\rho\alpha_{12}}^{\rho}+\frac{1}{2}g_{\alpha_{12}}\Delta_{1\rho}^{\rho}+\Delta_{1\alpha_{12}}+2g_{\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}}I_{\text{{quad}}}-2m^{2}\left(\Delta_{2\alpha_{12}}+g_{\alpha_{12}}I_{\log}\right)

(F.13)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\square_{2\rho\alpha_{12}}^{\rho}+\int\frac{\mathrm{d}^{2}k}{\left(2\pi\right)^{2}}\left[\frac{g_{\alpha_{12}}k^{2}}{D_{\lambda}}+\frac{2k_{\alpha_{12}}}{D_{\lambda}}\right]-2m^{2}\left(\Delta_{2\alpha_{12}}+g_{\alpha_{12}}I_{\log}\right)

(F.14)

\displaystyle\int\frac{\mathrm{d}^{2}k}{\left(2\pi\right)^{2}}\frac{2k_{\alpha_{12}}}{D_{\lambda}^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Delta_{2\alpha_{12}}+g_{\alpha_{12}}I_{\log}

(F.15)

\displaystyle\int\frac{\mathrm{d}^{2}k}{\left(2\pi\right)^{2}}\frac{2k_{\alpha_{12}}}{D_{\lambda}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Delta_{1\alpha_{12}}+g_{\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}}I_{\text{{quad}}}

(F.16)

\frac{1}{2}g_{\alpha_{12}}\Delta_{1\rho}^{\rho}+\Delta_{1\alpha_{12}}+2g_{\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}}I_{\text{{quad}}}=\int\frac{\mathrm{d}^{2}k}{\left(2\pi\right)^{2}}\left(g_{\alpha_{12}}k^{2}+2k_{\alpha_{12}}\right)\frac{1}{D_{\lambda}}

(F.17)

\square_{2\rho\alpha_{12}}^{\rho}=\int\frac{\mathrm{d}^{2}k}{\left(2\pi\right)^{2}}\left[4k^{2}k_{\alpha_{12}}-6k_{\alpha_{12}}D_{\lambda}-g_{\alpha_{12}}k^{2}D_{\lambda}\right]\frac{1}{D_{\lambda}^{2}}.

(F.18)

Where the quadratic form can be made null as

\Xi_{\alpha_{12}}^{\mathrm{quad}}=\int\frac{\mathrm{d}^{2}k}{\left(2\pi\right)^{2}}\left[4\left(k^{2}-m^{2}\right)-4D_{\lambda}\right]\frac{k_{\alpha_{12}}}{D_{\lambda}^{2}}=0.

(F.19)

F.2
Bilinears reductions and the accessible values to the uniqueness
factor

Observing the expressions

\displaystyle\Xi_{\nu_{23}}^{\left(a\right)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\int\frac{\mathrm{d}^{2}k}{\left(2\pi\right)^{2}}\left\{\left[\frac{8k^{2}k_{\nu_{23}}}{D_{\lambda}^{3}}-\frac{g_{\nu_{23}}k^{2}+6k_{\nu_{23}}}{D_{\lambda}^{2}}\right]\right.

(F.20)

\displaystyle-\left.\left[\frac{2k_{\nu_{23}}}{D_{\lambda}^{2}}-\frac{g_{\nu_{23}}}{D_{\lambda}}\right]-g_{\nu_{23}}\left[\frac{k^{2}}{D_{\lambda}^{2}}-\frac{1}{D_{\lambda}}\right]\right\}.

If it is linear and bilinears are reduced, follow the solid resu

\Xi_{\nu_{23}}^{\left(a\right)}=4m^{2}\int\frac{\mathrm{d}^{2}k}{\left(2\pi\right)^{2}}\left[\frac{4k_{\nu_{23}}}{D_{\lambda}^{3}}-g_{\nu_{23}}\frac{1}{D_{\lambda}^{2}}\right]=-4m^{2}\int\frac{\mathrm{d}^{2}k}{\left(2\pi\right)^{2}}\frac{\partial}{\partial k^{\nu_{3}}}\frac{k_{\nu_{2}}}{D_{\lambda}^{2}}\equiv 0.

(F.21)

The last passage involves defining a surface term that appears in 4D. Here
it is finite and indisputably zero.

As the higher rank term, they appear in the violations of RAGFs and unicity
of odd amplitudes

\Xi_{\mu_{1234}}^{\left(b\right)}=\left[3\Sigma_{4\rho\mu_{1234}}^{\rho}-8\square_{3\mu_{1234}}-g_{\mu_{1234}}\Delta_{2\rho}^{\rho}\right],

(F.22)

we will have for the first term

3g^{\mu_{12}}\Sigma_{4\mu_{123456}}=\int\frac{\mathrm{d}^{2}k}{\left(2\pi\right)^{2}}\left[\frac{144k^{2}k_{\mu_{3456}}}{D_{\lambda}^{4}}-\frac{8\left[10k_{\mu_{3456}}+k^{2}g_{(\mu_{34}}k_{\mu_{56})}\right]}{D_{\lambda}^{3}}\right],

(F.23)

using the formula
g^{\mu_{12}}g_{(\mu_{12}}k_{\mu_{3456})}=10k_{\mu_{3456}}+k^{2}g_{(\mu_{34}}k_{\mu_{56})}
and the definitions

\displaystyle 8\square_{3\mu_{3456}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int\frac{\mathrm{d}^{2}k}{\left(2\pi\right)^{2}}\left[\frac{64k_{\mu_{3456}}}{D_{\lambda}^{3}}-\frac{8g_{(\mu_{34}}k_{\mu_{56})}}{D_{\lambda}^{2}}\right]

(F.24)

\displaystyle g_{(\mu_{12}}g_{\mu_{34})}\Delta_{2\rho}^{\rho}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle g_{(\mu_{12}}g_{\mu_{34})}\int\frac{\mathrm{d}^{2}k}{\left(2\pi\right)^{2}}\left[\frac{2k^{2}}{D_{\lambda}^{2}}-\frac{2}{D_{\lambda}}\right],

(F.25)

it is obtained the result

\displaystyle\left[3g^{\mu_{12}}\Sigma_{4\mu_{123456}}-8\square_{3\mu_{3456}}-g_{(\mu_{12}}g_{\mu_{34})}\Delta_{2\rho}^{\rho}\right]

(F.26)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int\frac{\mathrm{d}^{2}k}{\left(2\pi\right)^{2}}\left\{\left[\frac{144k_{\mu_{3456}}}{D_{\lambda}^{3}}-\frac{8g_{(\mu_{34}}k_{\mu_{56})}}{D_{\lambda}^{2}}-\frac{2g_{(\mu_{12}}g_{\mu_{34})}}{D_{\lambda}}\right]\frac{k^{2}}{D_{\lambda}}\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 60.0pt\left.-\left[\frac{144k_{\mu_{3456}}}{D_{\lambda}^{3}}-\frac{8g_{(\mu_{34}}k_{\mu_{56})}}{D_{\lambda}^{2}}-\frac{2g_{(\mu_{12}}g_{\mu_{34})}}{D_{\lambda}}\right]\right\}.

Reducing bilinears by adding and subtracting the mass makes obtaining the
identity

\frac{k^{2}}{k^{2}-m^{2}}=1+\frac{m^{2}}{k^{2}-m^{2}}.

(F.27)

We reach at

\displaystyle\left[3g^{\mu_{12}}\Sigma_{4\mu_{123456}}-8\square_{3\mu_{3456}}-g_{(\mu_{12}}g_{\mu_{34})}\Delta_{2\rho}^{\rho}\right]

(F.28)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle m^{2}\int\frac{\mathrm{d}^{2}k}{\left(2\pi\right)^{2}}\left\{\frac{144k_{\mu_{3456}}}{D_{\lambda}^{4}}-\frac{8g_{(\mu_{34}}k_{\mu_{56})}}{D_{\lambda}^{3}}-\frac{2g_{(\mu_{12}}g_{\mu_{34})}}{D_{\lambda}^{2}}\right\}.

Mass terms do not vanish identically; what remains are precisely convergent
surface terms

\displaystyle\left[3g^{\mu_{12}}\Sigma_{4\mu_{123456}}-8\square_{3\mu_{3456}}-g_{(\mu_{12}}g_{\mu_{34})}\Delta_{2\rho}^{\rho}\right]

(F.29)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle m^{2}\int\frac{\mathrm{d}^{2}k}{\left(2\pi\right)^{2}}\left\{12\left[\frac{12k_{\mu_{3456}}}{D_{\lambda}^{4}}-\frac{g_{(\mu_{34}}k_{\mu_{56})}}{D_{\lambda}^{3}}\right]+4\frac{g_{(\mu_{34}}k_{\mu_{56})}}{D_{\lambda}^{3}}-\frac{g_{(\mu_{12}}g_{\mu_{34})}}{D_{\lambda}^{2}}-\frac{g_{(\mu_{12}}g_{\mu_{34})}}{D_{\lambda}^{2}}\right\},

these terms own integrands that are typical of four dimensions. Integrating
in 2D they are precisely zero

\Delta_{3;\mu_{ij}}=-\int\frac{\mathrm{d}^{2}k}{\left(2\pi\right)^{2}}\frac{\partial}{\partial k^{\mu_{i}}}\frac{k_{\mu_{j}}}{D_{\lambda}^{2}}=\int\frac{\mathrm{d}^{2}k}{\left(2\pi\right)^{2}}\left[\frac{4k_{\mu_{ij}}}{D_{\lambda}^{3}}-\frac{g_{\mu_{ij}}}{D_{\lambda}^{2}}\right]\equiv 0

(F.30)

\square_{4;\mu_{3456}}=-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{4}\int\frac{\mathrm{d}^{2}k}{\left(2\pi\right)^{2}}\frac{\partial}{\partial k^{\mu_{i}}}\frac{k_{\mu_{1}\cdots\hat{\mu}_{i}\cdots\mu_{4}}}{D_{\lambda}^{3}}=\int\frac{\mathrm{d}^{2}k}{\left(2\pi\right)^{2}}\left[\frac{12k_{\mu_{3456}}}{D_{\lambda}^{4}}-\frac{g_{(\mu_{34}}k_{\mu_{56})}}{D_{\lambda}^{3}}\right]\equiv 0

(F.31)

thereby

\left[3g^{\mu_{12}}\Sigma_{4\mu_{123456}}-8\square_{3\mu_{3456}}-g_{(\mu_{12}}g_{\mu_{34})}\Delta_{2\rho}^{\rho}\right]=m^{2}\left[12\square_{4\mu_{3456}}+g_{(\mu_{34}}\Delta_{3\mu_{56})}\right]=0

(F.32)

if the total derivative character of the expression is desired, we can also
write in the form

\displaystyle\left[3g^{\mu_{12}}\Sigma_{4\mu_{123456}}-8\square_{3\mu_{3456}}-g_{(\mu_{12}}g_{\mu_{34})}\Delta_{2\rho}^{\rho}\right]

(F.33)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle m^{2}\int\frac{\mathrm{d}^{2}k}{\left(2\pi\right)^{2}}\left\{-6\sum_{i=1}^{4}\frac{\partial}{\partial k^{\mu_{i}}}\frac{k_{\mu_{1}\cdots\hat{\mu}_{i}\cdots\mu_{4}}}{D_{\lambda}^{3}}-g_{(\mu_{3}\mu_{4}}\frac{\partial}{\partial k^{\mu_{5}}}\frac{k_{\mu_{6})}}{D_{\lambda}^{2}}\right\}.

Quadratic term in the Uniqueness factor:
We assume bilinear
reduction this term cancels identically independent from the definition of
the quadratic scalar

U_{\alpha_{12}}^{\mathrm{quad}}=\int\frac{\mathrm{d}^{2}k}{\left(2\pi\right)^{2}}\left\{\frac{16k^{2}k_{\alpha_{12}}}{D_{\lambda}^{2}}-\frac{16k_{\alpha_{12}}}{D_{\lambda}}\left[1-m^{2}\frac{1}{D_{\lambda}}\right]\right\}

(F.34)

in other words
U_{\alpha_{12}}^{\mathrm{quad}}=0
.
